```yaml
test_designs:
  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when a line break character is encountered in value printing.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void print(final Object value, final Appendable out, final boolean newRecord)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(final Appendable out, final Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with a value that contains a line break character.
      2. During printing, the print(...) method is reached, and the line break handling branch is taken.
      3. The output is formatted according to the line break handling logic.
    input_focus: |
      Provide a value that includes a line break character (e.g., "\n") to the printRecord() method.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the output correctly escapes or formats the line break according to the CSV specifications.

  - design_name: |
      Test handling of escape characters in value printing.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void printAndEscape(final CharSequence value, final int offset, final int len, final Appendable out)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(final Appendable out, final Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with a value that includes an escape character.
      2. During printing, the printAndEscape(...) method is reached, triggering the escape handling logic.
      3. The output is formatted with the escape character processed correctly.
    input_focus: |
      Provide a value that contains an escape character (e.g., "\\") to the printRecord() method.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the output reflects the correct escaping of the escape character in the printed value.

  - design_name: |
      Test quote handling for non-numeric values during printing.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void printAndQuote(final Object object, final CharSequence value, final int offset, final int len, final Appendable out, final boolean newRecord)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(final Appendable out, final Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with a non-numeric value that requires quoting.
      2. During printing, the printAndQuote(...) method is reached, and the quoting logic is executed.
      3. The output is formatted with the value encapsulated in quotes.
    input_focus: |
      Provide a non-numeric value that should be quoted (e.g., a string with special characters) to the printRecord() method.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the output includes the value correctly encapsulated in quotes as per the CSV formatting rules.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when the quote mode is set to non-numeric.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void printAndQuote(final Object object, final CharSequence value, final int offset, final int len, final Appendable out, final boolean newRecord)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(final Appendable out, final Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with a numeric value while the quote mode is set to non-numeric.
      2. During printing, the printAndQuote(...) method is reached, and the non-numeric branch is taken.
      3. The output is formatted without quotes for the numeric value.
    input_focus: |
      Set the quote mode to non-numeric and provide a numeric value to the printRecord() method.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the output does not include quotes around the numeric value, adhering to the non-numeric quote mode behavior.

  - design_name: |
      Test validation of duplicate headers in CSV format creation.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void validate()
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVFormat withHeader(String... header)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withHeader() with an array of header strings that contains duplicates.
      2. During header validation, the validate() method is reached, triggering the duplicate check.
      3. An IllegalArgumentException is expected due to the duplicate headers.
    input_focus: |
      Provide an array of header strings with at least one duplicate entry to the withHeader() method.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that an IllegalArgumentException is thrown, indicating the presence of duplicate headers.
```

```yaml
language: java
existing_test_function_signature: public void testWithNullString()
new_tests:
  - test_behavior: Test behavior when a line break character is encountered in value printing.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithLineBreak
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithLineBreak() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT;
          format.printRecord(writer, "Value1", "Value\nWithLineBreak", "Value3");
          String output = writer.toString();
          assertTrue(output.contains("Value1"));
          assertTrue(output.contains("Value\"nWithLineBreak"));
          assertTrue(output.contains("Value3"));
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

  - test_behavior: Test handling of escape characters in value printing.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithEscapeCharacter
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithEscapeCharacter() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT;
          format.printRecord(writer, "Value1", "Value with escape \\ character", "Value3");
          String output = writer.toString();
          assertTrue(output.contains("Value1"));
          assertTrue(output.contains("Value with escape \\\\ character"));
          assertTrue(output.contains("Value3"));
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

  - test_behavior: Test quote handling for non-numeric values during printing.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithNonNumericValue
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithNonNumericValue() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT;
          format.printRecord(writer, "Value1", "Value with special characters @#$%", "Value3");
          String output = writer.toString();
          assertTrue(output.contains("\"Value1\""));
          assertTrue(output.contains("\"Value with special characters @#$%\""));
          assertTrue(output.contains("\"Value3\""));
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

  - test_behavior: Test validation of duplicate headers in CSV format creation.
    test_name: testWithHeaderWithDuplicateEntries
    test_code: |
      @Test(expected = IllegalArgumentException.class)
      public void testWithHeaderWithDuplicateEntries() {
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withHeader("Column1", "Column2", "Column1");
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

```yaml
test_designs:
  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when a new record is created during printing.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void print(final Object value, final Appendable out, final boolean newRecord)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(final Appendable out, final Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with multiple values, ensuring the first value triggers newRecord as true.
      2. During printing, print(...) is reached and the newRecord branch is taken.
      3. The output is formatted with the first value treated as a new record.
    input_focus: |
      Provide an Appendable target and a list of values where the first value is distinct from the others.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the output correctly reflects the formatting rules applied to the first value as a new record.

  - design_name: |
      Test handling of characters that require quoting during printing.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void printAndQuote(final Object object, final CharSequence value, final int offset, final int len, final Appendable out, final boolean newRecord)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(final Appendable out, final Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with values that include special characters needing quotes.
      2. During printing, printAndQuote(...) is reached and the quoting logic is executed.
      3. The output is generated with appropriate quotes around the values.
    input_focus: |
      Include values that contain delimiters, line breaks, or quote characters.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the output correctly encapsulates values that require quoting according to the CSV format rules.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when trailing delimiters are enabled during printing.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void println(final Appendable out)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(final Appendable out, final Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with values and ensure trailing delimiters are set to true.
      2. During printing, println(...) is reached and the trailing delimiter logic is executed.
      3. The output includes the trailing delimiter after the record.
    input_focus: |
      Set the CSVFormat to allow trailing delimiters and provide a set of values to print.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the output ends with the specified delimiter followed by the record separator.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when the record separator is set to a custom value.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void print(final Object value, final Appendable out, final boolean newRecord)
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVFormat withRecordSeparator(final String recordSeparator)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withRecordSeparator() to set a custom record separator.
      2. Call printRecord() with values to trigger the printing process.
      3. The custom record separator is applied during output.
    input_focus: |
      Use a custom string as the record separator and provide a set of values to print.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the output uses the custom record separator instead of the default line breaks.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when escaping is enabled for special characters.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void printAndEscape(final CharSequence value, final int offset, final int len, final Appendable out)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(final Appendable out, final Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with values that include characters needing escaping.
      2. During printing, printAndEscape(...) is reached and the escaping logic is executed.
      3. The output is generated with appropriate escape characters for special values.
    input_focus: |
      Provide values that contain escape characters, delimiters, or line breaks.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the output correctly escapes special characters according to the CSV format rules.

    test_name: testPrintRecordNewRecord
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordNewRecord() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT;
          format.printRecord(writer, "FirstValue", "SecondValue", "ThirdValue");
          String output = writer.toString();
          assertTrue(output.startsWith("FirstValue"));
          assertTrue(output.contains("SecondValue"));
          assertTrue(output.contains("ThirdValue"));
      }
    new_imports_code: ''
    
  - test_behavior: Test handling of characters that require quoting during printing.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithSpecialCharacters
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithSpecialCharacters() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT;
          format.printRecord(writer, "Value1", "Value,WithComma", "Value\"WithQuote");
          String output = writer.toString();
          assertTrue(output.contains("\"Value1\""));
          assertTrue(output.contains("\"Value,WithComma\""));
          assertTrue(output.contains("\"Value\"WithQuote\""));
      }
    new_imports_code: ''
    
  - test_behavior: Test behavior when trailing delimiters are enabled during printing.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithTrailingDelimiter
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithTrailingDelimiter() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withTrailingDelimiter();
          format.printRecord(writer, "Value1", "Value2");
          String output = writer.toString();
          assertTrue(output.endsWith(",\n")); // Assuming default record separator is "\n"
      }
    new_imports_code: ''
    
  - test_behavior: Test behavior when the record separator is set to a custom value.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithCustomRecordSeparator
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithCustomRecordSeparator() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withRecordSeparator(";");
          format.printRecord(writer, "Value1", "Value2");
          String output = writer.toString();
          assertTrue(output.endsWith(";\n")); // Assuming default record separator is "\n"
      }
    new_imports_code: ''
    
  - test_behavior: Test behavior when escaping is enabled for special characters.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithEscapeCharacters
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithEscapeCharacters() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withEscape(BACKSLASH);
          format.printRecord(writer, "Value1", "Value with escape \\ character");
          String output = writer.toString();
          assertTrue(output.contains("Value1"));
          assertTrue(output.contains("Value with escape \\\\ character"));
      }

          format.printRecord(writer, "Value1", "Value\nWithLineBreak", "Value3");
          String output = writer.toString();
          assertTrue(output.contains("Value1"));
          assertTrue(output.contains("Value\nWithLineBreak"));
          assertTrue(output.contains("Value3"));
      }

          String output = writer.toString();

          assertTrue(output.contains("NULL"));
          assertTrue(output.contains("Value3"));

test_designs:
  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when a character is less than or equal to space.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private CharSequence trim(final CharSequence charSequence)
      entry_hint: |
        public String format(final Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call format() with values that include a string with leading spaces.
      2. Inside format(), trim(...) is reached when processing the string.
      3. The branch checking if the character is less than or equal to space is taken.
    input_focus: |
      Provide a string value that starts with spaces and includes other characters.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the leading spaces are trimmed correctly from the output string.

  - design_name: |
      Test trailing delimiter behavior in CSV output.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        public void printRecord(final Appendable out, final Object... values)
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVPrinter print(final Appendable out)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call print() with an Appendable target.
      2. Inside print(), printRecord(...) is invoked with multiple values.
      3. The branch checking for a trailing delimiter is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Use a CSVFormat instance configured to add a trailing delimiter and provide multiple values.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the output includes a trailing delimiter after the last value.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when the record separator is set to null.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        public CSVPrinter print(final Appendable out)
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVFormat withRecordSeparator(final String recordSeparator)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withRecordSeparator() with a null value.
      2. Inside print(), the recordSeparator check is reached.
      3. The branch that handles the null record separator is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Create a CSVFormat instance with a null record separator and invoke print().
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the output does not include any record separator.

  - design_name: |
      Test escape character handling in CSV output.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void printAndEscape(final CharSequence value, final int offset, final int len, final Appendable out)
      entry_hint: |
        public void print(final Object value, final Appendable out, final boolean newRecord)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call print() with a value that includes characters needing escape.
      2. Inside print(), printAndEscape(...) is invoked when the escape character is set.
      3. The branch checking if escaping is enabled is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Provide a value containing special characters that require escaping and ensure the escape character is set.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the output correctly escapes the special characters as per CSV formatting rules.

  - design_name: |
      Test comment marker behavior in CSV output.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void print(final Object value, final Appendable out, final boolean newRecord)
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVFormat withCommentMarker(final char commentMarker)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withCommentMarker() to set a comment marker.
      2. Inside print(), the check for the comment marker is reached.
      3. The branch that handles the presence of a comment marker is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Create a CSVFormat instance with a specific comment marker and invoke print() with a value that should trigger comment handling.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the output correctly formats comments according to the specified comment marker.

          String result = format.format("  LeadingSpaces  ", "Value2");
          assertFalse(result.startsWith("  "));
          assertFalse(result.contains("LeadingSpaces  "));
          assertTrue(result.contains("LeadingSpaces"));
      }

          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withTrailingDelimiter();
          format.printRecord(writer, "Value1", "Value2");
          String output = writer.toString();
          assertTrue(output.endsWith(","));

          assertFalse(output.contains("\r"));

```yaml
language: java
existing_test_function_signature: public void testWithHeaderUsingEnum()
new_tests:
  - test_behavior: Test behavior when header case sensitivity is ignored.
    test_name: testWithIgnoreHeaderCase
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testWithIgnoreHeaderCase() {
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withIgnoreHeaderCase(true);
          String csvInput = "Name,Value\nname,1\nNAME,2";
          CSVParser parser = format.parse(new StringReader(csvInput));
          List<CSVRecord> records = parser.getRecords();
          assertEquals("1", records.get(1).get("name")); // Accessing with lowercase
          assertEquals("2", records.get(2).get("NAME")); // Accessing with uppercase
          assertEquals("1", records.get(1).get("Name")); // Accessing with mixed case
      }
    new_imports_code: |
      import java.io.StringReader;
      import java.util.List;

  - test_behavior: Test handling of header comments during CSV parsing.
    test_name: testWithHeaderComments
    test_code: |
      @Test

```yaml
test_designs:
  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when no escape character is set and quote mode is NONE.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void validate()
    entry_hint: |
      public CSVFormat withEscape(Character escape)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withEscape() with a null escape character.
      2. The validate() method is invoked during the creation of the CSVFormat instance.
      3. The branch checking for the absence of an escape character and quote mode being NONE is taken.
    input_focus: |
      Create a CSVFormat instance with a null escape character and set quote mode to NONE.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that an IllegalArgumentException is thrown due to the invalid configuration.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when a header enum is provided.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        public CSVFormat withHeader(final Class<? extends Enum<?>> headerEnum)
    entry_hint: |
      public CSVFormat withHeader(String... header)

```yaml
language: java
existing_test_function_signature: public void testWithHeaderUsingEnum()
new_tests:
  - test_behavior: Test behavior when skipping the header record is enabled.
    test_name: testParseWithSkipHeaderRecord
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testParseWithSkipHeaderRecord() throws IOException {
          String csvInput = "Header1,Header2\nValue1,Value2\nValue3,Value4";
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withSkipHeaderRecord(true);
          CSVParser parser = format.parse(new StringReader(csvInput));
          List<CSVRecord> records = parser.getRecords();
          assertEquals(2, records.size()); // Ensure we have two records
          assertEquals("Value1", records.get(0).get(0)); // First record
          assertEquals("Value2", records.get(0).get(1)); // First record
          assertEquals("Value3", records.get(1).get(0)); // Second record
          assertEquals("Value4", records.get(1).get(1)); // Second record
      }
    new_imports_code: import java.io.StringReader; import java.util.List; import org.apache.commons.csv.CSVParser; import org.apache.commons.csv.CSVRecord;

  - test_behavior: Test behavior when comment marker is set.
    test_name: testParseWithCommentMarker
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testParseWithCommentMarker() throws IOException {
          String csvInput = "# This is a comment\nValue1,Value2\n# Another comment\nValue3,Value4";
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withCommentMarker('#');
          CSVParser parser = format.parse(new StringReader(csvInput));
          List<CSVRecord> records = parser.getRecords();
          assertEquals(2, records.size()); // Ensure we have two records
          assertEquals("Value1", records.get(0).get(0)); // First record
          assertEquals("Value2", records.get(0).get(1)); // First record
          assertEquals("Value3", records.get(1).get(0)); // Second record
          assertEquals("Value4", records.get(1).get(1)); // Second record
      }
    new_imports_code: import java.io.StringReader; import java.util.List; import org.apache.commons.csv.CSVParser; import org.apache.commons.csv.CSVRecord;

  - test_behavior: Test behavior when null string is defined.
    test_name: testParseWithNullString
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testParseWithNullString() throws IOException {
          String csvInput = "Column1,Column2\nValue1,NULL\nValue3,Value4";
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withNullString("NULL");
          CSVParser parser = format.parse(new StringReader(csvInput));
          List<CSVRecord> records = parser.getRecords();
          assertEquals(2, records.size()); // Ensure we have two records
          assertEquals("Value1", records.get(0).get(0)); // First record
          assertNull(records.get(0).get(1)); // Second column should be null
          assertEquals("Value3", records.get(1).get(0)); // Second record
          assertEquals("Value4", records.get(1).get(1)); // Second record
      }
    new_imports_code: import java.io.StringReader; import java.util.List; import org.apache.commons.csv.CSVParser; import org.apache.commons.csv.CSVRecord;

  - test_behavior: Test behavior when escape character is set.
    test_name: testParseWithEscapeCharacter
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testParseWithEscapeCharacter() throws IOException {
          String csvInput = "Column1,Column2\nValue1,Value\\,2\nValue3,Value4";
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withEscape('\\');
          CSVParser parser = format.parse(new StringReader(csvInput));
          List<CSVRecord> records = parser.getRecords();
          assertEquals(2, records.size()); // Ensure we have two records
          assertEquals("Value1", records.get(0).get(0)); // First record
          assertEquals("Value,2", records.get(0).get(1)); // Second column should be escaped
          assertEquals("Value3", records.get(1).get(0)); // Second record
          assertEquals("Value4", records.get(1).get(1)); // Second record
      }

test_designs:
  - design_name: |
      Test handling of line break characters in CSV values.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void printAndEscape(CharSequence value, int offset, int len, Appendable out)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(Appendable out, Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with a value containing line break characters (LF or CR).
      2. During printing, printAndEscape(...) is reached and the line break handling branch is taken.
      3. The output is generated with appropriate escaping for line breaks.
    input_focus: |
      Provide a value that includes both LF and CR characters to ensure they are handled correctly during printing.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the printed output correctly escapes the line break characters according to CSV formatting rules.

  - design_name: |
      Test quote handling for non-numeric values in CSV output.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void printAndQuote(Object object, CharSequence value, int offset, int len, Appendable out, boolean newRecord)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(Appendable out, Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with a non-numeric value that requires quoting.
      2. During printing, printAndQuote(...) is reached and the non-numeric branch is taken.
      3. The output is generated with the value encapsulated in quotes.
    input_focus: |
      Use a string value that contains special characters requiring quoting, such as commas or quotes.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the output correctly encapsulates the value in quotes and handles any internal quotes appropriately.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when trailing delimiters are enabled in CSV output.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void println(Appendable out)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(Appendable out, Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with multiple values, ensuring the trailing delimiter setting is enabled.
      2. During printing, println(...) is reached and the trailing delimiter branch is taken.
      3. The output includes a trailing delimiter after the last value.
    input_focus: |
      Set up a CSVFormat instance with trailing delimiters enabled and provide multiple values for printing.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the printed output includes a trailing delimiter after the last value in the record.

  - design_name: |
      Test escaping behavior for special characters in CSV output.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void printAndEscape(CharSequence value, int offset, int len, Appendable out)
      entry_hint: |
        public void printRecord(Appendable out, Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with a value containing special characters that need escaping.
      2. During printing, printAndEscape(...) is reached and the escaping branch is taken.
      3. The output is generated with special characters correctly escaped.
    input_focus: |
      Provide a value that includes special characters such as quotes, commas, or backslashes.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the output correctly escapes the special characters according to CSV formatting rules.

```yaml
language: java
existing_test_function_signature: public void testPrintRecordWithLineBreak()
new_tests:
  - test_behavior: Test handling of line break characters in CSV values.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithLineBreakCharacters
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithLineBreakCharacters() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT;
          format.printRecord(writer, "Value1", "Value\nWithLineBreak", "Value3");
          String output = writer.toString();
          assertTrue(output.contains("Value1"));
          assertTrue(output.contains("Value\nWithLineBreak"));
          assertTrue(output.contains("Value3"));
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

  - test_behavior: Test quote handling for non-numeric values in CSV output.
    test_name: testPrintRecordWithNonNumericValue
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintRecordWithNonNumericValue() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withQuote('"');
          format.printRecord(writer, "Value with special, characters");
          String output = writer.toString();
          assertTrue(output.contains("\"Value with special, characters\""));
      }
    new_imports_code: ''

    test_name: testPrintRecordWithTrailingDelimiter

          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withTrailingDelimiter();
          format.printRecord(writer, "Value1", "Value2");
          String output = writer.toString();
          assertTrue(output.endsWith(",\n")); // Check for trailing delimiter
      }

          format.printRecord(writer, "Value with \"quote\" and, comma");
          String output = writer.toString();

          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT;
          String result = format.format("   example   ");
          assertFalse(result.startsWith("   "));
          assertFalse(result.endsWith("   "));

          CSVFormat formatWithNoneQuoteMode = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withQuoteMode(QuoteMode.NONE);
          assertNotEquals(formatWithAllQuoteMode.hashCode(), formatWithNoneQuoteMode.hashCode());
      }

          CSVFormat formatWithoutIgnoreEmptyLines = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withIgnoreEmptyLines(false);
          assertNotEquals(formatWithIgnoreEmptyLines.hashCode(), formatWithoutIgnoreEmptyLines.hashCode());
      }

      public void testPrintAndQuoteWithAllQuoteMode() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withQuoteMode(QuoteMode.ALL);
          format.printRecord(writer, "Value with special, characters");
          String output = writer.toString();
          assertTrue(output.contains("\"Value with special, characters\""));
      }
    new_imports_code: ''
